31),\n]\nGAP_START_ROW = 35  # 35-40\nSTATS_ROWS = list(range(42, 48))  # 42-47\nWEIGHTED_ROW = 50\n\nCOLS = ['H','I','J','K','L']\n\nNS_MAIN = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'\nNS_REL = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships'\n\n\ndef detect_year_header_row(ws, cols=('H','I','J','K','L'), years=(2019,2020,2021,2022,2023), max_scan=50):\n    \"\"\"Find a row where the given columns match the year sequence.\"\"\"\n    for r in range(1, max_scan+1):\n        vals = [ws[f'{c}{r}'].value for c in cols]\n        if tuple(vals) == tuple(years):\n            return r\n    return None\n\n\ndef percentile_inc(values, k):\n    # Excel PERCENTILE (aka PERCENTILE.INC) with linear interpolation.\n    xs = sorted(values)\n    n = len(xs)\n    if n == 0:\n        raise ValueError('percentile on empty list')\n    if k <= 0:\n        return xs[0]\n    if k >= 1:\n        return xs[-1]\n    rank = 1 + (n - 1) * k\n    m = int(math.floor(rank))\n    d = rank - m\n    # m is 1-indexed position\n    if m <= 1:\n        return xs[0] + d * (xs[1] - xs[0])\n    if m >= n:\n        return xs[-1]\n    return xs[m-1] + d * (xs[m] - xs[m-1])\n\n\ndef fmt_number(v):\n    if v is None:\n        return ''\n    # Normalize floats very close to integers\n    if isinstance(v, (int,)):\n        return str(v)\n    if isinstance(v, float):\n        if abs(v - round(v)) < 1e-12:\n            return str(int(round(v)))\n        return format(v, '.15g')\n    # fall back\n    return str(v)\n\n\ndef addr(col, row):\n    return f'{col}{row}'\n\n\ndef build_cache_values():\n    # Load with formulas present or not doesn't matter for reading source numbers.\n    wb = load_workbook(IN_PATH, data_only=False)\n    ts = wb[TASK_SHEET]\n    ds = wb[DATA_SHEET]\n\n    year_row = detect_year_header_row(ds)\n    if year_row is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('Could not detect year header row on Data sheet in H-L')\n\n    years = [ds[f'{c}{year_row}'].value for c in COLS]\n\n    # Build lookup dict from Data rows 21-38 (as specified)\n    lookup = {}\n    for r in range(21, 39):\n        code = ds[f'D{r}'].value\n        if code is None:\n            continue\n        for c, y in zip(COLS, years):\n            val = ds[f'{c}{r}'].value\n            lookup[(code, y)] = val\n\n    # Read task years from Task row 10\n    task_years = [ts[f'{c}10'].value for c in COLS]\n\n    # Prepare caches\n    caches = {}\n\n    # Step 1 caches: lookups into H-L across the three blocks\n    for (r1, r2) in LOOKUP_BLOCKS:\n        for r in range(r1, r2+1):\n            code = ts[f'D{r}'].value\n            for c, y in zip(COLS, task_years):\n                caches[addr(c, r)] = lookup.get((code, y))\n\n    # Step 2 caches: gaps\n    for i in range(6):\n        r_success = 12 + i\n        r_fail = 19 + i\n        r_cap = 26 + i\n        r_gap = 35 + i\n        for c in COLS:\n            s = caches[addr(c, r_success)]\n            f = caches[addr(c, r_fail)]\n            cap = caches[addr(c, r_cap)]\n            if s is None or f is None or cap in (None, 0):\n                gap = None\n            else:\n                gap = (s - f) / cap * 100\n            caches[addr(c, r_gap)] = gap\n\n    # Step 2b caches: stats (map by labels)\n    stat_label_by_row = {r: ts[f'E{r}'].value for r in STATS_ROWS}\n\n    def stat_value(label, col_letter):\n        data = [caches[addr(col_letter, 35+i)] for i in range(6)]\n        # Ensure all numeric\n        if any(v is None for v in data):\n            return None\n        if label == 'Minimum':\n            return min(data)\n        if label == 'Maximum':\n            return max(data)\n        if label == 'Median':\n            return statistics.median(data)\n        if label == 'Simple mean':\n            return statistics.fmean(data)\n        if label == '25th percentile':\n            return percentile_inc(data, 0.25)\n        if label == '75th percentile':\n            return percentile_inc(data, 0.75)\n        raise KeyError(f'Unexpected stats label: {label!r}')\n\n    for r in STATS_ROWS:\n        label = stat_label_by_row[r]\n        for c in COLS:\n            caches[addr(c, r)] = stat_value(label, c)\n\n    # Step 3 caches: weighted mean\n    for c in COLS:\n        gaps = [caches[addr(c, 35+i)] for i in range(6)]\n        caps = [caches[addr(c, 26+i)] for i in range(6)]\n        if any(v is None for v in gaps) or any(v is None for v in caps) or sum(caps) == 0:\n            wm = None\n        else:\n            wm = sum(g*cap for g, cap in zip(gaps, caps)) / sum(caps)\n        caches[addr(c, WEIGHTED_ROW)] = wm\n\n    return caches, year_row\n\n\ndef write_formulas(year_header_row):\n    wb = load_workbook(IN_PATH, data_only=False)\n    ts = wb[TASK_SHEET]\n\n    # Step 1 - lookup formulas\n    # Note: year headers live in Data!H{year_header_row}:L{year_header_row}\n    for (r1, r2) in LOOKUP_BLOCKS:\n        for r in range(r1, r2+1):\n            for c in COLS:\n                formula = (\n                    f\"=INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38,\"\n                    f\"MATCH($D{r},Data!$D$21:$D$38,0),\"\n                    f\"MATCH({c}$10,Data!$H${year_header_row}:$L${year_header_row},0))\"\n                )\n                ts[f'{c}{r}'].value = formula\n\n    # Step 2 - net reliability gap\n    for i in range(6):\n        r_gap = 35 + i\n        r_success = 12 + i\n        r_fail = 19 + i\n        r_cap = 26 + i\n        for c in COLS:\n            ts[f'{c}{r_gap}'].value = f\"=({c}{r_success}-{c}{r_fail})/{c}{r_cap}*100\"\n\n    # Step 2b - summary stats (by label)\n    label_to_formula = {\n        'Minimum': 'MIN',\n        'Maximum': 'MAX',\n        'Median': 'MEDIAN',\n        'Simple mean': 'AVERAGE',\n        '25th percentile': 'PERCENTILE',\n        '75th percentile': 'PERCENTILE',\n    }\n\n    for r in STATS_ROWS:\n        label = ts[f'E{r}'].value\n        if label not in label_to_formula:\n            raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected stats label in E{r}: {label!r}')\n        func = label_to_formula[label]\n        for c in COLS:\n            rng = f\"{c}35:{c}40\"\n            if label == '25th percentile':\n                ts[f'{c}{r}'].value = f\"={func}({rng},0.25)\"\n            elif label == '75th percentile':\n                ts[f'{c}{r}'].value = f\"={func}({rng},0.75)\"\n            else:\n                ts[f'{c}{r}'].value = f\"={func}({rng})\"\n\n    # Step 3 - weighted mean\n    for c in COLS:\n        ts[f'{c}{WEIGHTED_ROW}'].value = f\"=SUMPRODUCT({c}35:{c}40,{c}26:{c}31)/SUM({c}26:{c}31)\"\n\n    wb.save(OUT_PATH)\n\n\ndef map_sheet_to_xml(zipf):\n    # Read workbook.xml and relationships to find the XML filename for Task sheet\n    wb_xml = zipf.read('xl/workbook.xml')\n    rels_xml = zipf.read('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels')\n\n    wb_root = etree.fromstring(wb_xml)\n    rels_root = etree.fromstring(rels_xml)\n\n    ns = {'m': NS_MAIN, 'r': NS_REL}\n\n    sheet_id = None\n    for sh in wb_root.xpath('.//m:sheets/m:sheet', namespaces=ns):\n        if sh.get('name') == TASK_SHEET:\n            sheet_id = sh.get(f'{{{NS_REL}}}id')\n            break\n    if sheet_id is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('Could not find Task sheet in workbook.xml')\n\n    target = None\n    for rel in rels_root.xpath('.//r:Relationship', namespaces={'r': 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships'}):\n        if rel.get('Id') == sheet_id:\n            target = rel.get('Target')\n            break\n    if target is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('Could not resolve Task sheet relationship target')\n\n    # Target is relative to xl/ (often 'worksheets/sheet1.xml')\n    if target.startswith('/'):\n        target = target.lstrip('/')\n    if not target.startswith('worksheets/'):\n        # could be worksheets/sheet1.xml already; else still relative\n        target = f'{target}'\n\n    sheet_path = 'xl/' + target\n    return sheet_path\n\n\ndef inject_cached_values(caches):\n    # Modify Task sheet XML in the saved OUT_PATH zip\n    tmp_path = OUT_PATH + '.tmp'\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, 'r') as zin:\n        sheet_path = map_sheet_to_xml(zin)\n        sheet_xml = zin.read(sheet_path)\n\n        root = etree.fromstring(sheet_xml)\n        ns = {'m': NS_MAIN}\n\n        def set_cell_value(cell_ref, number_value):\n            c_el = root.xpath(f\".//m:c[@r='{cell_ref}']\", namespaces=ns)\n            if not c_el:\n                # If the cell element doesn't exist, create it under the right row.\n                # This should be rare because Task sheet likely already has these cells.\n                row_num = int(''.join(ch for ch in cell_ref if ch.isdigit()))\n                row_el = root.xpath(f\".//m:row[@r='{row_num}']\", namespaces=ns)\n                if not row_el:\n                    sheetData = root.find(f'{{{NS_MAIN}}}sheetData')\n                    row_el = etree.SubElement(sheetData, f'{{{NS_MAIN}}}row', r=str(row_num))\n                else:\n                    row_el = row_el[0]\n                c_el = etree.SubElement(row_el, f'{{{NS_MAIN}}}c', r=cell_ref)\n            else:\n                c_el = c_el[0]\n\n            # Ensure numeric type\n            if 't' in c_el.attrib:\n                c_el.attrib.pop('t', None)\n\n            v_el = c_el.find(f'{{{NS_MAIN}}}v')\n            if v_el is None:\n                v_el = etree.SubElement(c_el, f'{{{NS_MAIN}}}v')\n            v_el.text = fmt_number(number_value)\n\n        # Only inject into our known target cells\n        targets = set(caches.keys())\n        for cell_ref in targets:\n            set_cell_value(cell_ref, caches[cell_ref])\n\n        new_sheet_xml = etree.tostring(root, xml_declaration=True, encoding='UTF-8', standalone=None)\n\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(tmp_path, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            for item in zin.infolist():\n                if item.filename == sheet_path:\n                    zout.writestr(item, new_sheet_xml)\n                else:\n                    zout.writestr(item,       print(f'-- {label} ({range_str}) --')\n        for row in ts[range_str]:\n            print([c.value for c in row])\n\n    dump('H12:L17', 'data_only H12:L17')\n    dump('H19:L24', 'data_only H19:L24')\n    dump('H26:L31', 'data_only H26:L31')\n    dump('H35:L40', 'data_only H35:L40')\n    dump('H42:L47', 'data_only H42:L47')\n    dump('H50:L50', 'data_only H50:L50')\n\n    wb_f = print('H42:', tsf['H42'].value)\n    print('H50:', tsf['H50'].value)\n\n\n# --- Execute ---\ncache_vals, year_row = build_cache_values()\nwrite_formulas(year_row)\n# rebuild caches based on source; ok Build lookup from Data rows 21-38 (D codes; H-L values)\n    lookup = {}\n    for r in range(21,39):\n        code = ds[f'D{r}'].value\n        if code is None:\n            continue\n        for c,y in zip(COLS, years):\n            lookup[(code,y)] = ds[f'{c}{r}'].value\n\n    task_years = [ts[f'{c}10'].value for c in COLS]\n\n    caches = {}\n\n    # Stats rows 42-47 (label-driven)\n    labels = {r: ts[f'E{r}'].value for r in STATS_ROWS}\n\n    def stat(label, col):\n        xs = [caches[addr(col,35+i)] for i in range(6)]\n        if label == 'Minimum':\n            return min(xs)\n        if label == 'Maximum':\n            return max(xs)\n        if label == 'Median':\n            return statistics.median(xs)\n        if label == 'Simple mean':\n            return statistics.fmean(xs)\n        if label == '25th percentile':\n            return percentile_inc(xs, 0.25)\n        if label == '75th percentile':\n            return percentile_inc(xs, 0.75)\n        raise RuntimeError(f'Unknown stats label {label!r}')\n\n    for r in STATS_ROWS:\n        label = labels[r]\n        for c in COLS:\n            caches[addr(c,r)] = stat(label, c)\n\n    #
